^SAMPLE = GSM7510899
!Sample_title = Pre-treatment blood of patient P5 (R/PR)
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM7510899
!Sample_status = Public on Dec 17 2024
!Sample_submission_date = Jun 26 2023
!Sample_last_update_date = Dec 17 2024
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = HBV+ HCC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: blood
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = patient: P5
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = response: PR, responder
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: CD45+ immune cells
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = library type: scRNA-seq & scTCR-seq
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: anti-PD-1 plus lenvatinib combination therapy
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To perform single-cell sequencing, single-cell suspensions from tumor and blood samples were stained with antibodies against CD45 and sorted using a BD FACSAriaTM III instrument. Based on FACS analysis, single cells were sorted into 15 ml tubes (Nest) and manually counted under a microscope. To perform bulk RNA analysis, small fragments of tumor tissues were first preserved in RNAlater RNA stabilization reagent (QIAGEN) immediately after surgical resection, and stored on ice to prevent RNA degradation. The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_description = 10X
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data
!Sample_data_processing = For scTCR-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) with the manufacturer-supplied human VDJ reference genome (refdata-cellranger-vdj-GRCh38-alts-ensembl-5.0.0). For each sample, the output file all_contig_annotations.csv, containing TCR α- and β-chain complementarity determining region 3 (CDR3) amino acid sequence, was used for downstream analysis. These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: h5ad files for expression matrices
!Sample_platform_id = GPL24676
!Sample_contact_name = Chen,,Dong
!Sample_contact_institute = Westlake University
!Sample_contact_address = Shilongshan Street No.18, Xihu District
!Sample_contact_city = Hangzhou
!Sample_contact_state = Zhejiang
!Sample_contact_zip/postal_code = 310024
!Sample_contact_country = China
!Sample_instrument_model = Illumina NovaSeq 6000
!Sample_library_selection = cDNA
!Sample_library_source = transcriptomic
!Sample_library_strategy = RNA-Seq
!Sample_supplementary_file_1 = NONE
!Sample_series_id = GSE235863
!Sample_data_row_count = 0

These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.

!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = library type: scRNA-seq & scTCR-seq
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: anti-PD-1 plus lenvatinib combination therapy
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To perform single-cell sequencing, single-cell suspensions from tumor and blood samples were stained with antibodies against CD45 and sorted using a BD FACSAriaTM III instrument. Based on FACS analysis, single cells were sorted into 15 ml tubes (Nest) and manually counted under a microscope. To perform bulk RNA analysis, small fragments of tumor tissues were first preserved in RNAlater RNA stabilization reagent (QIAGEN) immediately after surgical resection, and stored on ice to prevent RNA degradation. The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_description = 10X
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data

The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_description = 10X
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data
!Sample_data_processing = For scTCR-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) with the manufacturer-supplied human VDJ reference genome (refdata-cellranger-vdj-GRCh38-alts-ensembl-5.0.0). For each sample, the output file all_contig_annotations.csv, containing TCR α- and β-chain complementarity determining region 3 (CDR3) amino acid sequence, was used for downstream analysis. These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: h5ad files for expression matrices

!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.

!Sample_data_row_count = 0
^SAMPLE = GSM7510913
!Sample_title = Post-treatment blood of patient P27 (R/PR)
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM7510913
!Sample_status = Public on Dec 17 2024
!Sample_submission_date = Jun 26 2023
!Sample_last_update_date = Dec 17 2024
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = HBV+ HCC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: blood
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = patient: P27
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = response: PR, responder
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: CD45+ immune cells
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = library type: scRNA-seq & scTCR-seq
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: anti-PD-1 plus lenvatinib combination therapy
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To perform single-cell sequencing, single-cell suspensions from tumor and blood samples were stained with antibodies against CD45 and sorted using a BD FACSAriaTM III instrument. Based on FACS analysis, single cells were sorted into 15 ml tubes (Nest) and manually counted under a microscope. To perform bulk RNA analysis, small fragments of tumor tissues were first preserved in RNAlater RNA stabilization reagent (QIAGEN) immediately after surgical resection, and stored on ice to prevent RNA degradation. The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_description = 10X
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data
!Sample_data_processing = For scTCR-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) with the manufacturer-supplied human VDJ reference genome (refdata-cellranger-vdj-GRCh38-alts-ensembl-5.0.0). For each sample, the output file all_contig_annotations.csv, containing TCR α- and β-chain complementarity determining region 3 (CDR3) amino acid sequence, was used for downstream analysis. These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: h5ad files for expression matrices

These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = library type: scRNA-seq & scTCR-seq
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: anti-PD-1 plus lenvatinib combination therapy
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To perform single-cell sequencing, single-cell suspensions from tumor and blood samples were stained with antibodies against CD45 and sorted using a BD FACSAriaTM III instrument. Based on FACS analysis, single cells were sorted into 15 ml tubes (Nest) and manually counted under a microscope. To perform bulk RNA analysis, small fragments of tumor tissues were first preserved in RNAlater RNA stabilization reagent (QIAGEN) immediately after surgical resection, and stored on ice to prevent RNA degradation. The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_description = 10X
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data

The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_description = 10X
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data
!Sample_data_processing = For scTCR-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) with the manufacturer-supplied human VDJ reference genome (refdata-cellranger-vdj-GRCh38-alts-ensembl-5.0.0). For each sample, the output file all_contig_annotations.csv, containing TCR α- and β-chain complementarity determining region 3 (CDR3) amino acid sequence, was used for downstream analysis. These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.

!Sample_contact_zip/postal_code = 310024
!Sample_contact_country = China
!Sample_instrument_model = Illumina NovaSeq 6000
!Sample_library_selection = cDNA
!Sample_library_source = transcriptomic
!Sample_library_strategy = RNA-Seq
!Sample_supplementary_file_1 = NONE
!Sample_series_id = GSE235863
!Sample_data_row_count = 0
^SAMPLE = GSM7510928
!Sample_title = CD45+CD8- immune cells from pre-treatment tumor of patient P16 (NR/PD)
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM7510928
!Sample_status = Public on Dec 17 2024
!Sample_submission_date = Jun 26 2023
!Sample_last_update_date = Dec 17 2024
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = HBV+ HCC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: liver tumor
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = patient: P16
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = response: PD, non-responder
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: CD45+ immune cells exclude CD45+CD3+CD8+ cells
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = library type: scRNA-seq & scTCR-seq & deCODE-Dextramer-seq
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: anti-PD-1 plus lenvatinib combination therapy
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To perform single-cell sequencing, single-cell suspensions from tumor and blood samples were stained with antibodies against CD45 and sorted using a BD FACSAriaTM III instrument. Based on FACS analysis, single cells were sorted into 15 ml tubes (Nest) and manually counted under a microscope. To perform bulk RNA analysis, small fragments of tumor tissues were first preserved in RNAlater RNA stabilization reagent (QIAGEN) immediately after surgical resection, and stored on ice to prevent RNA degradation. The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_description = 10X
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data
!Sample_data_processing = For scTCR-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) with the manufacturer-supplied human VDJ reference genome (refdata-cellranger-vdj-GRCh38-alts-ensembl-5.0.0). For each sample, the output file all_contig_annotations.csv, containing TCR α- and β-chain complementarity determining region 3 (CDR3) amino acid sequence, was used for downstream analysis. These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: h5ad files for expression matrices

Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data
!Sample_data_processing = For scTCR-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) with the manufacturer-supplied human VDJ reference genome (refdata-cellranger-vdj-GRCh38-alts-ensembl-5.0.0). For each sample, the output file all_contig_annotations.csv, containing TCR α- and β-chain complementarity determining region 3 (CDR3) amino acid sequence, was used for downstream analysis. These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: h5ad files for expression matrices

!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = HBV+ HCC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: liver tumor
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = patient: P16
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = response: PD, non-responder
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: CD45+ immune cells exclude CD45+CD3+CD8+ cells
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = library type: scRNA-seq & scTCR-seq & deCODE-Dextramer-seq
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: anti-PD-1 plus lenvatinib combination therapy
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To perform single-cell sequencing, single-cell suspensions from tumor and blood samples were stained with antibodies against CD45 and sorted using a BD FACSAriaTM III instrument. Based on FACS analysis, single cells were sorted into 15 ml tubes (Nest) and manually counted under a microscope. To perform bulk RNA analysis, small fragments of tumor tissues were first preserved in RNAlater RNA stabilization reagent (QIAGEN) immediately after surgical resection, and stored on ice to prevent RNA degradation. The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: CD45+ immune cells exclude CD45+CD3+CD8+ cells
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = library type: scRNA-seq & scTCR-seq & deCODE-Dextramer-seq
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: anti-PD-1 plus lenvatinib combination therapy
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To perform single-cell sequencing, single-cell suspensions from tumor and blood samples were stained with antibodies against CD45 and sorted using a BD FACSAriaTM III instrument. Based on FACS analysis, single cells were sorted into 15 ml tubes (Nest) and manually counted under a microscope. To perform bulk RNA analysis, small fragments of tumor tissues were first preserved in RNAlater RNA stabilization reagent (QIAGEN) immediately after surgical resection, and stored on ice to prevent RNA degradation. The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_description = 10X
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data
!Sample_data_processing = For scTCR-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) with the manufacturer-supplied human VDJ reference genome (refdata-cellranger-vdj-GRCh38-alts-ensembl-5.0.0). For each sample, the output file all_contig_annotations.csv, containing TCR α- and β-chain complementarity determining region 3 (CDR3) amino acid sequence, was used for downstream analysis. These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: h5ad files for expression matrices

These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38

!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = HBV+ HCC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: liver tumor
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = patient: P3
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = response: PR, responder

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = library type: scRNA-seq & scTCR-seq & deCODE-Dextramer-seq
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: anti-PD-1 plus lenvatinib combination therapy
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To perform single-cell sequencing, single-cell suspensions from tumor and blood samples were stained with antibodies against CD45 and sorted using a BD FACSAriaTM III instrument. Based on FACS analysis, single cells were sorted into 15 ml tubes (Nest) and manually counted under a microscope. To perform bulk RNA analysis, small fragments of tumor tissues were first preserved in RNAlater RNA stabilization reagent (QIAGEN) immediately after surgical resection, and stored on ice to prevent RNA degradation. The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_description = 10X
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.

!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data
!Sample_data_processing = For scTCR-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) with the manufacturer-supplied human VDJ reference genome (refdata-cellranger-vdj-GRCh38-alts-ensembl-5.0.0). For each sample, the output file all_contig_annotations.csv, containing TCR α- and β-chain complementarity determining region 3 (CDR3) amino acid sequence, was used for downstream analysis. These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.

!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: liver tumor
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = patient: P32
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = response: PR, responder
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = library type: Bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: anti-PD-1 plus lenvatinib combination therapy
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To perform single-cell sequencing, single-cell suspensions from tumor and blood samples were stained with antibodies against CD45 and sorted using a BD FACSAriaTM III instrument. Based on FACS analysis, single cells were sorted into 15 ml tubes (Nest) and manually counted under a microscope. To perform bulk RNA analysis, small fragments of tumor tissues were first preserved in RNAlater RNA stabilization reagent (QIAGEN) immediately after surgical resection, and stored on ice to prevent RNA degradation. The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data
!Sample_data_processing = For scTCR-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) with the manufacturer-supplied human VDJ reference genome (refdata-cellranger-vdj-GRCh38-alts-ensembl-5.0.0). For each sample, the output file all_contig_annotations.csv, containing TCR α- and β-chain complementarity determining region 3 (CDR3) amino acid sequence, was used for downstream analysis. These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: h5ad files for expression matrices

!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.

!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = library type: Bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: anti-PD-1 plus lenvatinib combination therapy
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To perform single-cell sequencing, single-cell suspensions from tumor and blood samples were stained with antibodies against CD45 and sorted using a BD FACSAriaTM III instrument. Based on FACS analysis, single cells were sorted into 15 ml tubes (Nest) and manually counted under a microscope. To perform bulk RNA analysis, small fragments of tumor tissues were first preserved in RNAlater RNA stabilization reagent (QIAGEN) immediately after surgical resection, and stored on ice to prevent RNA degradation. The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data
!Sample_data_processing = For scTCR-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) with the manufacturer-supplied human VDJ reference genome (refdata-cellranger-vdj-GRCh38-alts-ensembl-5.0.0). For each sample, the output file all_contig_annotations.csv, containing TCR α- and β-chain complementarity determining region 3 (CDR3) amino acid sequence, was used for downstream analysis. These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.

!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data
!Sample_data_processing = For scTCR-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) with the manufacturer-supplied human VDJ reference genome (refdata-cellranger-vdj-GRCh38-alts-ensembl-5.0.0). For each sample, the output file all_contig_annotations.csv, containing TCR α- and β-chain complementarity determining region 3 (CDR3) amino acid sequence, was used for downstream analysis. These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: h5ad files for expression matrices

^SAMPLE = GSM8626324
!Sample_title = Post-treatment liver tumor of patient P52 (NR/SD)
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM8626324
!Sample_status = Public on Dec 17 2024
!Sample_submission_date = Nov 12 2024
!Sample_last_update_date = Dec 17 2024
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = HBV+ HCC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = tissue: liver tumor
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = patient: P52
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = response: NR/SD
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = library type: scRNA-seq & scTCR-seq
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: anti-PD-1 plus lenvatinib combination therapy
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To perform single-cell sequencing, single-cell suspensions from tumor and blood samples were stained with antibodies against CD45 and sorted using a BD FACSAriaTM III instrument. Based on FACS analysis, single cells were sorted into 15 ml tubes (Nest) and manually counted under a microscope. To perform bulk RNA analysis, small fragments of tumor tissues were first preserved in RNAlater RNA stabilization reagent (QIAGEN) immediately after surgical resection, and stored on ice to prevent RNA degradation. The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq

!Sample_data_row_count = 0
^SAMPLE = GSM8626328
!Sample_title = CD45+CD3+CD8+ immune cells from post-treatment tumor of patient P57 (R)
!Sample_geo_accession = GSM8626328
!Sample_status = Public on Dec 17 2024
!Sample_submission_date = Nov 12 2024
!Sample_last_update_date = Dec 17 2024
!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = HBV+ HCC
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Homo sapiens
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 9606
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = patient: P57
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = response: R
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cell type: CD45+CD3+CD8+ immune cells
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = library type: scRNA-seq & scTCR-seq & deCODE-Dextramer-seq
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = treatment: anti-PD-1 plus lenvatinib combination therapy
!Sample_treatment_protocol_ch1 = All patients included in this study were pathologically diagnosed with HBV-infected hepatocellular carcinoma and received lenvatinib (12 mg/day for bodyweight ≥ 60 kg or 8 mg/day for bodyweight < 60 kg) orally once daily and a PD-1 inhibitor intravenously on day 1 of a 21-day treatment cycle. The anti-PD-1 antibodies and their single-dose infusion were sintilimab 200 mg, pembrolizumab 200 mg, tislelizumab 200 mg, or toripalimab 240 mg.
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To perform single-cell sequencing, single-cell suspensions from tumor and blood samples were stained with antibodies against CD45 and sorted using a BD FACSAriaTM III instrument. Based on FACS analysis, single cells were sorted into 15 ml tubes (Nest) and manually counted under a microscope. To perform bulk RNA analysis, small fragments of tumor tissues were first preserved in RNAlater RNA stabilization reagent (QIAGEN) immediately after surgical resection, and stored on ice to prevent RNA degradation. The RNA was then extracted from the tumor tissue samples using the RNeasy Mini Kit (QIAGEN), following the manufacturer's specifications. The concentration of RNA was quantified using the NanoDrop instrument (Thermo), and the quality of RNA was evaluated using the fragment analyzer (AATI).
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = To construct scRNA-seq libraries, the concentration of single cell suspensions was adjusted to 1,000 cells/ul, and cells were loaded between 5,000 and 20,000 cells/reaction using 10X Chromium Single Cell 5' and human variable, diversity, and joining (VDJ) (10X Genomics). All the subsequent steps were performed following the standard manufacturer’s protocols. Purified libraries were analyzed by an Illumina NovaSeq 6000 sequencer with 150-bp paired-end reads. The bulk RNA-seq libraries were constructed using the NEBNext Poly (A) mRNA Magnetic Isolation Module kit (NEB) and the NEBNext Ultra RNA Library Prep Kit for Illumina Paired-end Multiplexed Sequencing Library (NEB). Finally, the samples were sequenced on the Illumina Hiseq 4000 sequencer with 150 bp paired-end reads.
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library; 10x Chromium Single cell 5’ + VDJ library + deCODE dextramer library; bulk RNA-seq
!Sample_description = 10X
!Sample_description = GSE235863_five_patients_scRNAseq_cd8t_raw_counts.h5ad.gz
!Sample_description = GSE235863_five_patients_TCR.csv.gz
!Sample_description = GSE235863_five_patients_dCODE_dextramer_counts.csv.gz
!Sample_data_processing = For scRNA-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using the Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) against the GRCh38 human reference genome (refdata-gex-GRCh38-2020-A). Preliminary filtered raw count data generated from Cell Ranger were used for downstream analysis. Further quality control was applied to filter out low-quality cells with either (1) <500 expressed genes, (2) >6,000 expressed genes, (3) >20% mitochondrial UMI counts, or <1% hemoglobin UMI counts (sum of UMI counts of HBA1, HBA2 and HBB genes) and filter out genes detected in less than 3 cells. Scrublet (v.0.2.3) was applied to each sequencing library to remove potential doublets with default setting.
!Sample_data_processing = For 10X dCODE dextramer experiments, data
!Sample_data_processing = For scTCR-seq samples, reads were aligned and quantified using Cell Ranger Single-Cell toolkit (v.6.1.2) with the manufacturer-supplied human VDJ reference genome (refdata-cellranger-vdj-GRCh38-alts-ensembl-5.0.0). For each sample, the output file all_contig_annotations.csv, containing TCR α- and β-chain complementarity determining region 3 (CDR3) amino acid sequence, was used for downstream analysis. These files were combined using scirpy (v.0.11.0) and quality control was performed with the following criterions: 1) low-confidence, non-productive chains or those with UMIs < 2 were discarded; 2) cells with more than four productive chains: two VJ and two VDJ chains were discarded; 3) cells with only one VD or only one VDJ chain were discarded; 4) for T cells with two α- or β- chains assembled, the α-β pair showing the highest expression level (UMIs) was defined as the domain α- β pair in the corresponding T cells. For each patient, T cells comprising the same VJ and VDJ CDR3 amino acid sequences for each patient were considered as having originated from the same clone and were therefore identified as clonal cells.
!Sample_data_processing = For bulk RNA-seq samples, we applied FastQC (v.0.11.9) to determine the quality of the raw sequencing data. Adapters, low quality bases and reads were removed using 'Trim Galore' (v.0.6.5). Then, the expression levels of transcripts were quantified by Salmon (v.1.9.1). The gene expression levels of genes, including raw count and transcript per million (TPM), were generated by tximport (v.1.18.0) with the inputs from Salmon.
!Sample_data_processing = Assembly: hg38
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: csv and txt files for TCR summary, and deCODE dextramer summary.
!Sample_data_processing = Supplementary files format and content: h5ad files for expression matrices
